FEATURE-Account-Delete.md ===\n# FEATURE: Account Delete\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Delete an account that has no transactions and print the deleted code. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-Code-Validation.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md |\n| Provides    | ledger account delete <code> |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, accounts.delete, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger account delete <code>` removes an account. The delete is refused while any transaction\nreferences the account.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- The `<code>` argument is validated as an account code (FEATURE-Code-Validation.md) before any lookup.\n- The account is looked up with `accounts.get`. An absent code exits 4 with no change.\n- If any transaction references the account, the command exits 3 with no change.\n- Otherwise the account is removed with `accounts.delete` and the output is:\n\n  ```text\n  deleted\n  <code>\n  ```\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger account delete <code>` | `deleted`, code | account in use | absent code |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: retrieve an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-005` — write: delete an account - supplies: `account` - returns: `rows`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to detect references - supplies: none - returns: `account`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-delete-unused-succeeds ===\nIntent: Deleting an account with no transactions prints deleted and the code, and the row is gone.\nSuite: scoped\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"CASH9\", name=\"Event Float\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", \"CASH9\")\n    row = db.accounts.get(account=\"CASH9\")\n\nassert code == 0\nassert out == \"deleted\\nCASH9\\n\"\nassert row is None\n=== END AC account-delete-unused-succeeds ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-in-use-exits-3 ===\nIntent: Deleting an account referenced by a transaction exits 3 and leaves the account in place.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"CASH9\", name=\"Event Float\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=1, date=\"2026-01-01\", account=\"CASH9\", category=\"SALES\", amount=100, memo=\"\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", \"CASH9\")\n    row = db.accounts.get(account=\"CASH9\")\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\nassert row.name == \"Event Float\"\n=== END AC account-delete-in-use-exits-3 ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-absent-exits-4 ===\nIntent: Deleting an absent account exits 4 and writes nothing to stdout.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", \"NOSUCH\")\n\nassert code == 4\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC account-delete-absent-exits-4 ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The in-use check is computed by query over transactions, never by a stored count.\n- Application code reaches the database only through `persistence` operations.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transaction-Post.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Post\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Post a transaction with the next id after validating every field and applying the balance rule. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-Code-Validation.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Date-Validation.md, FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>], next_txn_id() |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, categories.get, transactions.list, transactions.create |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn post` records one transaction against an account and category. It assigns the next\ntransaction id, validates every field, applies the non-negative balance rule, and prints the new row.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- Argument syntax is validated before any lookup. A malformed amount, date, code, or memo exits 3\n  even when the account is absent (AC-010).\n- The amount is parsed as integer cents (FEATURE-Amount-Format.md). Zero in any form exits 3.\n- The date is validated as a real `YYYY-MM-DD` calendar date (FEATURE-Date-Validation.md).\n- The memo is optional, 0 to 60 characters, with no comma or newline. An omitted memo is stored as\n  the empty string.\n- The account and category are looked up with `accounts.get` and `categories.get`. A missing account\n  or category exits 3, not 4 (AC-011).\n- `next_txn_id()` returns the largest `txn_id` from `transactions.list` plus one, or `1` when the\n  table is empty. Ids are computed by query.\n- The balance rule (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md) is checked after the insert and before commit. A\n  resulting negative balance exits 3 with no change.\n- On success the row is written with `transactions.create` and printed as:\n\n  ```text\n  txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\n  <id>,<date>,<account>,<category>,<amount>,<memo>\n  ```\n\n  The amount uses the two-decimal output format.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]` | new row | malformed argument, missing account or category, negative balance | not used |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: retrieve an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-002` — read: retrieve a category by code - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to compute the next id and balances - supplies: none - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-001` — write: create a transaction - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-assigns-next-id ===\nIntent: Posting into a populated table assigns the largest txn_id plus one and prints the new row.\nSuite: scoped\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7, date=\"2026-01-01\", account=\"DRAWER1\", category=\"SALES\", amount=500, memo=\"\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"DRAWER1\", \"SALES\", \"45.50\", \"2026-04-01\", \"walk in sale\")\n    row = db.transactions.get(txn_id=8)\n\nassert code == 0\nassert out == \"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\\n8,2026-04-01,DRAWER1,SALES,45.50,walk in sale\\n\"\nassert row.amount == 4550\n=== END AC txn-post-assigns-next-id ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-empty-table-starts-at-1 ===\nIntent: Posting into an empty transactions table assigns txn_id 1.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"DRAWER1\", \"SALES\", \"10.00\", \"2026-04-01\")\n    row = db.transactions.get(txn_id=1)\n\nassert code == 0\nassert row.account == \"DRAWER1\"\nassert row.amount == 1000\n=== END AC txn-post-empty-table-starts-at-1 ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-zero-amount-exits-3 ===\nIntent: A zero amount in any form is rejected with exit 3 and stores nothing.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"DRAWER1\", \"SALES\", \"-0.00\", \"2026-04-01\")\n    count = db.transactions.list()\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(count) == 0\n=== END AC txn-post-zero-amount-exits-3 ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-malformed-amount-before-lookup ===\nIntent: A malformed amount exits 3 even when the account is absent (AC-010).\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"NOSUCH\", \"SALES\", \"1.234\", \"2026-04-01\")\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC txn-post-malformed-amount-before-lookup ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-missing-account-exits-3 ===\nIntent: Posting to an account that does not exist exits 3, not 4 (AC-011).\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"NOSUCH\", \"SALES\", \"1.00\", \"2026-04-01\")\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC txn-post-missing-account-exits-3 ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-invalid-date-exits-3 ===\nIntent: An impossible calendar date is rejected with exit 3.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"DRAWER1\", \"SALES\", \"1.00\", \"2026-02-30\")\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC txn-post-invalid-date-exits-3 ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-negative-balance-exits-3 ===\nIntent: A post that would leave an account below zero exits 3 and stores nothing.\n\nfrom db.categories.create(category=\"SUPPLY\", description=\"Supplies\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"PETTY\", \"SUPPLY\", \"-1000.00\", \"2026-04-04\")\n    count = db.transactions.list()\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(count) == 0\n=== END AC txn-post-negative-balance-exits-3 ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The next id, balances, and printed values are computed by query; no value is hardcoded.\n- Amounts are integer cents end to end; no floating point is used for money.\n- Application code reaches the database only through `persistence` operations.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transaction-Get.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Get\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Amend\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Change only the amount of one transaction, applying the balance rule and printing the updated row. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn amend <id> <amount> |\n| Consumes    | transactions.get, transactions.update, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn amend <id> <amount>` replaces the amount of an existing transaction. No other field\nchanges.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- The `<id>` argument must be an integer. A non-integer id exits 3 before any lookup.\n- The `<amount>` argument is parsed as integer cents (FEATURE-Amount-Format.md). Zero in any form\n  exits 3.\n- The transaction is read with `transactions.get`. An absent id exits 4 with no change.\n- The amount is written with `transactions.update`. No other column changes.\n- The balance rule (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md) is checked after the update and before commit. A\n  resulting negative balance exits 3 with no change.\n- On success the updated row is printed as:\n\n  ```text\n  txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\n  <id>,<date>,<account>,<category>,<amount>,<memo>\n  ```\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger txn amend <id> <amount>` | updated row | malformed id or amount, negative balance | absent id |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: retrieve a transaction by id - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-004` — write: update a transaction amount - supplies: `txn_id`, `amount` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to compute balances - supplies: none - returns: `account`, `amount`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-amend-changes-only-amount ===\nIntent: Amending a transaction changes only its amount and prints the updated row.\nSuite: scoped\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=121, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=\"DRAWER1\", category=\"SALES\", amount=4550, memo=\"walk in sale\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"amend\", \"121\", \"54.50\")\n    row = db.transactions.get(txn_id=121)\n\nassert code == 0\nassert out == \"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\\n121,2026-04-01,DRAWER1,SALES,54.50,walk in sale\\n\"\nassert row.amount == 5450\nassert row.memo == \"walk in sale\"\nassert row.date == \"2026-04-01\"\n=== END AC txn-amend-changes-only-amount ===\n\n=== AC txn-amend-absent-exits-4 ===\nIntent: Amending an absent transaction id exits 4 and writes nothing to stdout.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"amend\", \"999\", \"1.00\")\n\nassert code == 4\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC txn-amend-absent-exits-4 ===\n\n=== AC txn-amend-zero-amount-exits-3 ===\nIntent: A zero amount in any form is rejected with exit 3 and the stored amount is unchanged.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front row.amount == 4550\n=== END AC txn-amend-zero-amount-exits-3 ===\n\n=== AC txn-amend-negative-balance-exits-3 ===\nIntent: An amendment that would leave an account below zero exits 3 and changes It validates the\ncode and the new name, looks up the account, updates its name, and prints the updated row.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- The `<code>` argument is validated as an account code (FEATURE-Code-Validation.md) before any lookup.\n- The `<name>` argument is validated as an account name: 1 to 40 characters, no comma, no newline.\n  An invalid name exits 3 with no change.\n- The account is looked up with `accounts.get`. An absent code exits 4 with no change.\n- On success the name is written with `accounts.update` and the updated row is printed through the\n  CSV output contract (FEATURE-CLI-Output.md) as:\n\n  ```text\n  account,name,transactions,balance\n  <code>,<new name>,<transaction count>,<balance>\n  ```\n\n- The transaction count and balance are computed from the account's transactions by query, never\n  embedded.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger account rename <code> <name>` | updated row | invalid code or name | absent code |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: retrieve an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-004` — write: update account name - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-rename-updates-name ===\nIntent: Renaming an existing account stores the new name and the FEATURE-Account-Delete.md ===\n# FEATURE: Account Delete\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Delete an account that has no transactions and print the deleted code. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-Code-Validation.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md |\n| Provides    | ledger account delete <code> |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, accounts.delete, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger account delete <code>` removes an account. The delete is refused while any transaction\nreferences the account.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- The `<code>` argument is validated as an account code (FEATURE-Code-Validation.md) before any lookup.\n- The account is looked up with `accounts.get`. An absent code exits 4 with no change.\n- If any transaction references the account, the command exits 3 with no change.\n- Otherwise the account is removed with `accounts.delete` and the output is:\n\n  ```text\n  deleted\n  <code>\n  ```\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger account delete <code>` | `deleted`, code | account in use | absent code |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: retrieve an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-005` — write: delete an account - supplies: `account` - returns: `rows`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to detect references - supplies: none - returns: `account`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC account-delete-unused-succeeds ===\nIntent: Deleting an account with no transactions prints deleted and the code, and the row is gone.\nSuite: scoped\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"CASH9\", name=\"Event Float\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", \"CASH9\")\n    row = db.accounts.get(account=\"CASH9\")\n\nassert code == 0\nassert out == \"deleted\\nCASH9\\n\"\nassert row is None\n=== END AC account-delete-unused-succeeds ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-in-use-exits-3 ===\nIntent: Deleting an account referenced by a transaction exits 3 and leaves the account in place.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"CASH9\", name=\"Event Float\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=1, date=\"2026-01-01\", account=\"CASH9\", category=\"SALES\", amount=100, memo=\"\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", \"CASH9\")\n    row = db.accounts.get(account=\"CASH9\")\n\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\nassert row.name == \"Event Float\"\n=== END AC account-delete-in-use-exits-3 ===\n\n=== AC account-delete-absent-exits-4 ===\nIntent: Deleting an absent account exits 4 and writes nothing to stdout.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", \"NOSUCH\")\n\nassert code == 4\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC account-delete-absent-exits-4 ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The in-use check is computed by query over transactions, never by a stored count.\n- Application code reaches the database only through `persistence` operations.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Transaction-Post.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Post\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Post a transaction with the next id after validating every field and applying the balance rule. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-Code-Validation.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Date-Validation.md, FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>], next_txn_id() |\n| Consumes    | accounts.get, categories.get, transactions.list, transactions.create |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn post` records one transaction against an account and category. It assigns the next\ntransaction id, validates every field, applies the non-negative balance rule, and prints the new row.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- Argument syntax is validated before any lookup. A malformed amount, date, code, or memo exits 3\n  even when the account is absent (AC-010).\n- The amount is parsed as integer cents (FEATURE-Amount-Format.md). Zero in any form exits 3.\n- The date is validated as a real `YYYY-MM-DD` calendar date (FEATURE-Date-Validation.md).\n- The memo is optional, 0 to 60 characters, with no comma or newline. An omitted memo is stored as\n  the empty string.\n- The account and category are looked up with `accounts.get` and `categories.get`. A missing account\n  or category exits 3, not 4 (AC-011).\n- `next_txn_id()` returns the largest `txn_id` from `transactions.list` plus one, or `1` when the\n  table is empty. Ids are computed by query.\n- The balance rule (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md) is checked after the insert and before commit. A\n  resulting negative balance exits 3 with no change.\n- On success the row is written with `transactions.create` and printed as:\n\n  ```text\n  txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\n  <id>,<date>,<account>,<category>,<amount>,<memo>\n  ```\n\n  The amount uses the two-decimal output format.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]` | new row | malformed argument, missing account or category, negative balance | not used |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: retrieve an account by code - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-002` — read: retrieve a category by code - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to compute the next id and balances - supplies: none - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-001` — write: create a transaction - supplies: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-assigns-next-id ===\nIntent: Posting into a populated table assigns the largest txn_id plus one and prints the new row.\nSuite: scoped\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=7, date=\"2026-01-01\", account=\"DRAWER1\", category=\"SALES\", amount=500, memo=\"\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", \"DRAWER1\", \"SALES\", \"45.50\", \"2026-04-01\", \"walk in sale\")\n    row = db.transactions.get(txn_id=8)\n\nassert code == 0\nassert out == \"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\\n8,2026-04-01,DRAWER1,SALES,45.50,walk in sale\\n\"\nassert row.amount == 4550\n=== END AC txn-post-assigns-next-id ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-empty-table-starts-at-1 ===\nIntent: Posting into an empty transactions table assigns txn_id 1.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith Amend\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Change only the amount of one transaction, applying the balance rule and printing the updated row. |\n| Depends On  | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md, FEATURE-CLI-Output.md, FEATURE-Amount-Format.md, FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md, FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md |\n| Provides    | ledger txn amend <id> <amount> |\n| Consumes    | transactions.get, transactions.update, transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger txn amend <id> <amount>` replaces the amount of an existing transaction. No other field\nchanges.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The command runs inside one command transaction (FEATURE-Command-Transaction.md).\n- The `<id>` argument must be an integer. A non-integer id exits 3 before any lookup.\n- The `<amount>` argument is parsed as integer cents (FEATURE-Amount-Format.md). Zero in any form\n  exits 3.\n- The transaction is read with `transactions.get`. An absent id exits 4 with no change.\n- The amount is written with `transactions.update`. No other column changes.\n- The balance rule (FEATURE-Balance-Rule.md) is checked after the update and before commit. A\n  resulting negative balance exits 3 with no change.\n- On success the updated row is printed as:\n\n  ```text\n  txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\n  <id>,<date>,<account>,<category>,<amount>,<memo>\n  ```\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n| Command | Exit 0 | Exit 3 | Exit 4 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ledger txn amend <id> <amount>` | updated row | malformed id or amount, negative balance | absent id |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: retrieve a transaction by id - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-004` — write: update a transaction amount - supplies: `txn_id`, `amount` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list transactions to compute balances - supplies: none - returns: `account`, `amount`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-amend-changes-only-amount ===\nIntent: Amending a transaction changes only its amount and prints the updated row.\nSuite: scoped\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=\"DRAWER1\", name=\"Front Drawer\")\n    db.categories.create(category=\"SALES\", description=\"Sales receipts\")\n    db.transactions.create(txn_id=121, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=\"DRAWER1\", category=\"SALES\", amount=4550, memo=\"walk in sale\")\n    code, out, _ = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"amend\", \"121\", \"54.50\")\n    row = db.transactions.get(txn_id=121)\n\nassert code == 0\nassert out == \"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\\n121,2026-04-01,DRAWER1,SALES,54.50,walk in sale\\n\"\nassert row.amount == 5450\nassert row.memo == \"walk in sale\"\nassert row.date == \"2026-04-01\"\n=== END AC txn-amend-changes-only-amount ===\n\n=== AC txn-amend-absent-exits-4 ===\nIntent: Amending an absent transaction id exits 4
